<details><summary>reference</summary>

```verilog

module RefModule (
  input clk,
  input load,
  input [1:0] ena,
  input [99:0] data,
  output reg [99:0] q
);

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (load)
      q <= data;
    else if (ena == 2'h1)
      q <= {q[0], q[99:1]};
    else if (ena == 2'h2)
      q <= {q[98:0], q[99]};
  end

endmodule
```

</details>

#### attempt 1 — pass
**rtl**
```verilog
module TopModule (
    input clk,
    input load,
    input [1:0] ena,
    input [99:0] data,
    output reg [99:0] q
);

always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (load) begin
        q <= data;
    end else begin
        case (ena)
            2'b01: q <= {q[0], q[99:1]}; // Rotate right
            2'b10: q <= {q[98:0], q[99]}; // Rotate left
            default: q <= q; // No rotation
        endcase
    end
end

endmodule
```

);

<details><summary>reference</summary>

```verilog

module RefModule (
  input clk,
  input areset,
  input bump_left,
  input bump_right,
  output walk_left,
  output walk_right
);

  parameter WL=0, WR=1;
  reg state;
  reg next;

  always_comb begin
    case (state)
      WL: next = bump_left ? WR : WL;
      WR: next = bump_right ? WL: WR;
    endcase
  end

  always @(posedge clk, posedge areset) begin
    if (areset) state <= WL;
      else state <= next;
  end

  assign walk_left = (state==WL);
  assign walk_right = (state==WR);

endmodule
```

</details>

#### attempt 1 — pass
**rtl**
```verilog
module TopModule (
    input  clk,
    input  areset,
    input  bump_left,
    input  bump_right,
    output walk_left,
    output walk_right
);

    reg state;
    reg next_state;

    parameter LEFT = 1'b0;
    parameter RIGHT = 1'b1;

    always @(posedge clk or posedge areset) begin
        if (areset)
            state <= LEFT;
        else
            state <= next_state;
    end

    always @(*) begin
        case (state)
            LEFT: begin
                if (bump_left || (bump_left && bump_right))
                    next_state = RIGHT;
                else
                    next_state = LEFT;
            end
            RIGHT: begin
                if (bump_right || (bump_left && bump_right))
                    next_state = LEFT;
                else
                    next_state = RIGHT;
            end
            default: next_state = LEFT;
        endcase
    end

    assign walk_left = (state == LEFT);
    assign walk_right = (state == RIGHT);

endmodule
```

module RefModule (
  input clk,
  input areset,
  input bump_left,
  input bump_right,
  input ground,
  output walk_left,
  output walk_right,
  output aaah
);

  parameter WL=0, WR=1, FALLL=2, FALLR=3;
  reg [1:0] state;
  reg [1:0] next;

  always_comb begin
    case (state)
      WL: next = ground ? (bump_left ? WR : WL) : FALLL;
      WR: next = ground ? (bump_right ? WL: WR) : FALLR;
      FALLL: next = ground ? WL : FALLL;
      FALLR: next = ground ? WR : FALLR;
    endcase
  end

  always @(posedge clk, posedge areset) begin
    if (areset) state <= WL;
      else state <= next;
  end

  assign walk_left = (state==WL);
  assign walk_right = (state==WR);
  assign aaah = (state == FALLL) || (state == FALLR);

endmodule
```

module TopModule (
    input clk,
    input areset,
    input bump_left,
    input bump_right,
    input ground,
    output walk_left,
    output walk_right,
    output aaah
);

    typedef enum logic [1:0] {
        LEFT = 2'b01,
        RIGHT = 2'b10,
        FALLING = 2'b11
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;

    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge areset) begin
        if (areset) begin
            state <= LEFT;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
        end
    end

    always_comb begin
        next_state = state;
        case (state)
            LEFT: begin
                if (bump_left) begin
                    next_state = RIGHT;
                end else if (!ground) begin
                    next_state = FALLING;
                end
            end
            RIGHT: begin
                if (bump_right) begin
                    next_state = LEFT;
                end else if (!ground) begin
                    next_state = FALLING;
                end
            end
            FALLING: begin
                if (ground) begin
                    next_state = state;
                end
            end
        endcase
    end

    assign walk_left = (state == LEFT);
    assign walk_right = (state == RIGHT);
    assign aaah = (state == FALLING);

endmodule

```verilog
module TopModule (
    input  clk,
    input  areset,
    input  bump_left,
    input  bump_right,
    input  ground,
    output reg walk_left,
    output reg walk_right,
    output reg aaah
);

    typedef enum logic [1:0] {
        LEFT = 2'b01,
        RIGHT = 2'b10,
        FALLING = 2'b11
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;

    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge areset) begin
        if (areset) begin
            state <= LEFT;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
        end
    end

    always_comb begin
        case (state)
            LEFT: begin
                if (bump_left) begin
                    next_state = RIGHT;
                end else if (!ground) begin
                    next_state = FALLING;
                end else begin
                    next_state = LEFT;
                end
            end
            RIGHT: begin
                if (bump_right) begin
                    next_state = LEFT;
                end else if (!ground) begin
                    next_state = FALLING;
                end else begin
                    next_state = RIGHT;
                end
            end
            FALLING: begin
                if (ground) begin
                    next_state = state;
                end else begin
                    next_state = FALLING;
                end
            end
            default: begin
                next_state = LEFT;
            end
        endcase
    end

    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin
        walk_left <= (state == LEFT);
        walk_right <= (state == RIGHT);
        aaah <= (state == FALLING);
    end

endmodule

  input in,
  output disc,
  output flag,
  output err
);

      S2: state <= in ? S3 : S0;
      S3: state <= in ? S4 : S0;

      S6: state <= in ? SERR : SFLAG;
      SERR: state <= in ? SERR : S0;
      SFLAG: state <= in ? S1 : S0;
      SDISC: state <= in ? S1 : S0;
      default: state <= 'x;
    endcase

    if (reset) state <= S0;
  end

endmodule

    output reg err
);

    typedef enum reg [2:0] {
        IDLE = 3'b000,
        ONE = 3'b001,
        TWO = 3'b010,
        THREE = 3'b011,
        FOUR = 3'b100,
        FIVE = 3'b101,
        SIX = 3'b110,
        SEVEN = 3'b111
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            disc <= 0;
            flag <= 0;
            err <= 0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            disc <= 0;
            flag <= 0;
            err <= 0;
            case (state)
                IDLE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = ONE;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                ONE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = TWO;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                TWO: begin
                    if (in) next_state = THREE;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                THREE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = FOUR;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                FOUR: begin
                    if (in) next_state = FIVE;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                FIVE: begin

                        next_state = IDLE;
                        disc <= 1;

                        flag <= 1;

                        next_state = SEVEN;
                        err <= 1;
                    end else begin
                        next_state = IDLE;
                        flag <= 1;
                    end
                end
                default: next_state = IDLE;
            endcase

    end

endmodule

module TopModule (
    input clk,
    input reset,
    input in,
    output reg disc,
    output reg flag,
    output reg err
);

    typedef enum reg [2:0] {
        IDLE = 3'b000,
        ONE = 3'b001,
        TWO = 3'b010,
        THREE = 3'b011,
        FOUR = 3'b100,
        FIVE = 3'b101,
        SIX = 3'b110,
        SEVEN = 3'b111
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            disc <= 0;
            flag <= 0;
            err <= 0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            disc <= 0;
            flag <= 0;
            err <= 0;
            case (state)
                IDLE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = ONE;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                ONE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = TWO;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                TWO: begin
                    if (in) next_state = THREE;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                THREE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = FOUR;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                FOUR: begin
                    if (in) next_state = FIVE;
                    else next_state = IDLE;
                end
                FIVE: begin
                    if (in) next_state = SIX;
                    else begin
                        next_state = IDLE;
                        disc <= 1;
                    end
                end
                SIX: begin
                    if (in) next_state = SEVEN;
                    else begin
                        next_state = IDLE;
                        flag <= 1;
                    end
                end
                SEVEN: begin
                    if (in) begin
                        next_state = SEVEN;
                        err <= 1;
                    end else begin
                        next_state = IDLE;
                        flag <= 1;
                    end
                end
                default: next_state = IDLE;
            endcase
        end
    end

endmodule

        endcase
    end

module TopModule (
    input  clk,
    input  reset,
    input  in,
    output disc,
    output flag,
    output err
);

    reg [2:0] state;
    reg [2:0] next_state;
    reg disc_reg;
    reg flag_reg;
    reg err_reg;

    parameter S0 = 3'b000;
    parameter S1 = 3'b001;
    parameter S2 = 3'b010;
    parameter S3 = 3'b011;
    parameter S4 = 3'b100;
    parameter S5 = 3'b101;
    parameter S6 = 3'b110;
    parameter S7 = 3'b111;

    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= S0;
            disc_reg <= 0;
            flag_reg <= 0;
            err_reg <= 0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            disc_reg <= (next_state == S6);
            flag_reg <= (next_state == S7);
            err_reg <= (next_state == S7);
        end
    end

    always @(*) begin
        case (state)
            S0: next_state = (in) ? S1 : S0;
            S1: next_state = (in) ? S2 : S0;
            S2: next_state = (in) ? S3 : S0;
            S3: next_state = (in) ? S4 : S0;

```verilog

module RefModule (
  input clk,
  input reset,
  input data,
  output reg shift_ena,
  output reg counting,
  input done_counting,
  output reg done,
  input ack
);

  typedef enum logic[3:0] {
    S, S1, S11, S110, B0, B1, B2, B3, Count, Wait
  } States;

  States state, next;

  always_comb begin
    case (state)
      S: next = States'(data ? S1: S);
      S1: next = States'(data ? S11: S);
      S11: next = States'(data ? S11 : S110);
      S110: next = States'(data ? B0 : S);
      B0: next = B1;
      B1: next = B2;
      B2: next = B3;
      B3: next = Count;
      Count: next = States'(done_counting ? Wait : Count);
      Wait: next = States'(ack ? S : Wait);
      default: next = States'(4'bx);
    endcase
  end

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (reset) state <= S;
    else state <= next;
  end

  always_comb begin
    shift_ena = 0; counting = 0; done = 0;
    if (state == B0 || state == B1 || state == B2 || state == B3)
      shift_ena = 1;
    if (state == Count)
      counting = 1;
    if (state == Wait)
      done = 1;

    if (|state === 1'bx) begin
      {shift_ena, counting, done} = 'x;
    end

  end

endmodule
```

```verilog
module TopModule (
    input  clk,
    input  reset,
    input  data,
    input  done_counting,
    input  ack,
    output shift_ena,
    output counting,
    output done
);

    typedef enum logic [2:0] {
        IDLE,
        DETECT_1,
        DETECT_11,
        DETECT_110,
        SHIFT,
        COUNT,
        DONE_WAIT
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;
    logic [1:0] shift_counter;
    logic shift_ena_reg, counting_reg, done_reg;

    always_ff @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            shift_counter <= 2'b00;
            shift_ena_reg <= 1'b0;
            counting_reg <= 1'b0;
            done_reg <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            shift_counter <= (state == SHIFT && shift_counter < 2'b10) ? shift_counter + 1 : 2'b00;
        end
    end

    always_comb begin
        next_state = state;
        shift_ena_reg = 1'b0;
        counting_reg = 1'b0;
        done_reg = 1'b0;

        case (state)
            IDLE: begin
                if (data == 1'b1)
                    next_state = DETECT_1;
            end
            DETECT_1: begin
                if (data == 1'b1)
                    next_state = DETECT_11;
                else
                    next_state = IDLE;
            end
            DETECT_11: begin
                if (data == 1'b0)
                    next_state = DETECT_110;
                else
                    next_state = IDLE;
            end
            DETECT_110: begin
                if (data == 1'b1)
                    next_state = SHIFT;
                else
                    next_state = IDLE;
            end
            SHIFT: begin
                shift_ena_reg = 1'b1;
                if (shift_counter == 2'b10)
                    next_state = COUNT;
            end
            COUNT: begin
                counting_reg = 1'b1;
                if (done_counting)
                    next_state = DONE_WAIT;
            end
            DONE_WAIT: begin
                done_reg = 1'b1;
                if (ack)
                    next_state = IDLE;
            end
        endcase
    end

    assign shift_ena = shift_ena_reg;
    assign counting = counting_reg;
    assign done = done_reg;

endmodule
```

            SHIFT: begin

            COUNT: begin

                if (done_counting)
                    next_state = WAIT_ACK;
            end
            WAIT_ACK: begin
                done = 1'b1;
                if (ack)
                    next_state = IDLE;
            end
        endcase
    end

```verilog

module RefModule (
  input wire clk,
  input wire reset,
  input wire data,
  output wire [3:0] count,
  output reg counting,
  output reg done,
  input wire ack
);

  typedef enum logic[3:0] {
    S, S1, S11, S110, B0, B1, B2, B3, Count, Wait
  } States;

  States state, next;

  reg shift_ena;
  reg [9:0] fcount;
  reg [3:0] scount;
  wire done_counting = (scount == 0) && (fcount == 999);

  always_comb begin
    case (state)
      S: next = States'(data ? S1: S);
      S1: next = States'(data ? S11: S);
      S11: next = States'(data ? S11 : S110);
      S110: next = States'(data ? B0 : S);
      B0: next = B1;
      B1: next = B2;
      B2: next = B3;
      B3: next = Count;
      Count: next = States'(done_counting ? Wait : Count);
      Wait: next = States'(ack ? S : Wait);
      default: next = States'(4'bx);
    endcase
  end

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (reset) state <= S;
    else state <= next;
  end

  always_comb begin
    shift_ena = 0; counting = 0; done = 0;
    if (state == B0 || state == B1 || state == B2 || state == B3)
      shift_ena = 1;
    if (state == Count)
      counting = 1;
    if (state == Wait)
      done = 1;

    if (|state === 1'bx) begin
      {shift_ena, counting, done} = 'x;
    end
  end

  // Shift register
  always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (shift_ena)
      scount <= {scount[2:0], data};
    else if (counting && fcount == 999)
      scount <= scount - 1'b1;
  end

  // Fast counter
  always @(posedge clk)
    if (!counting)
      fcount <= 10'h0;
    else if (fcount == 999)
      fcount <= 10'h0;
    else
      fcount <= fcount + 1'b1;

  assign count = counting ? scount : 'x;

endmodule
```

module TopModule (

    input  wire ack
);

        IDLE,
        DETECT_PATTERN,
        SHIFT_DELAY,
        COUNTDOWN,
        WAIT_ACK
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;
    reg [3:0] pattern;
    reg [3:0] delay;
    reg [9:0] counter;

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            pattern <= 4'b0000;
            delay <= 4'b0000;
            counter <= 10'd0;
            count <= 4'b0000;
            counting <= 1'b0;
            done <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            case (state)
                IDLE: begin
                    if (data)
                        pattern <= {pattern[2:0], data};
                    else
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    if (pattern == 4'b1101)

                    else

                    next_state <= SHIFT_DELAY;

                    delay <= {delay[2:0], data};

                    counter <= counter + 1'd1;

                        next_state <= IDLE;
                        done <= 1'b0;
                    end else
                        next_state <= WAIT_ACK;
                end
                default: begin
                    next_state <= IDLE;
                end
            endcase

    end

endmodule

    input  wire data,
    output reg [3:0] count,
    output reg counting,
    output reg done,
    input  wire ack
);

    typedef enum reg [2:0] {
        IDLE,
        DETECT_PATTERN,
        SHIFT_DELAY,
        COUNTDOWN,
        WAIT_ACK
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;
    reg [3:0] pattern;
    reg [3:0] delay;
    reg [9:0] counter;
    reg [1:0] shift_counter;

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            pattern <= 4'b0000;
            delay <= 4'b0000;
            counter <= 10'd0;
            count <= 4'b0000;
            counting <= 1'b0;
            done <= 1'b0;
            shift_counter <= 2'b00;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            case (state)
                IDLE: begin
                    if (data == 1'b1) begin
                        pattern <= {pattern[2:0], data};
                    end else begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end
                end
                DETECT_PATTERN: begin
                    pattern <= 4'b0000;
                end
                SHIFT_DELAY: begin
                    if (shift_counter < 2'b10) begin
                        delay <= {delay[2:0], data};
                        shift_counter <= shift_counter + 1'b1;
                    end
                end
                COUNTDOWN: begin
                    if (counter != 10'd0) begin
                        counter <= counter - 1'b1;
                    end else begin
                        if (count != 4'b0000) begin
                            count <= count - 1'b1;
                            counter <= 10'd999;
                        end
                    end
                end
                WAIT_ACK: begin
                    if (ack) begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                        shift_counter <= 2'b00;
                    end
                end
            endcase
        end
    end

    always @(*) begin
        next_state = state;
        case (state)
            IDLE: begin
                if (pattern == 4'b1101) begin
                    next_state = DETECT_PATTERN;
                end
            end
            DETECT_PATTERN: begin
                next_state = SHIFT_DELAY;
            end
            SHIFT_DELAY: begin
                if (shift_counter == 2'b10) begin
                    next_state = COUNTDOWN;
                    count <= delay;
                    counter <= 10'd999;
                end
            end
            COUNTDOWN: begin
                if (count == 4'b0000 && counter == 10'd0) begin
                    next_state = WAIT_ACK;
                end
            end
            WAIT_ACK: begin
                if (ack) begin
                    next_state = IDLE;
                end
            end
        endcase
    end

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            counting <= 1'b0;
            done <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            counting <= (state == COUNTDOWN);
            done <= (state == WAIT_ACK);
        end
    end

endmodule

module TopModule (
    input  clk,
    input  reset,
    input  data,
    output reg [3:0] count,
    output reg counting,
    output reg done,
    input  ack
);

    typedef enum reg [2:0] {
        IDLE,
        DETECT_PATTERN,
        SHIFT_DELAY,
        COUNTDOWN,
        WAIT_ACK
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;
    reg [3:0] pattern;
    reg [3:0] delay;
    reg [9:0] counter;

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            pattern <= 4'b0000;
            delay <= 4'b0000;
            counter <= 10'd0;
            count <= 4'b0000;
            counting <= 1'b0;
            done <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            case (state)
                IDLE: begin
                    if (data == 1'b1) begin
                        pattern <= {pattern[2:0], data};
                    end else begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end
                end
                DETECT_PATTERN: begin
                    if (pattern == 4'b1101) begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end
                end
                SHIFT_DELAY: begin
                    delay <= {delay[2:0], data};
                end
                COUNTDOWN: begin
                    if (counter != 10'd0) begin
                        counter <= counter - 10'd1;
                    end else begin
                        if (count != 4'b0000) begin
                            count <= count - 4'b0001;
                            counter <= 10'd999;
                        end
                    end
                end
                WAIT_ACK: begin
                    if (ack) begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end
                end
            endcase
        end
    end

    always @(*) begin
        next_state = state;
        case (state)
            IDLE: begin
                if (pattern == 4'b1101) begin
                    next_state = DETECT_PATTERN;
                end
            end
            DETECT_PATTERN: begin
                next_state = SHIFT_DELAY;
            end
            SHIFT_DELAY: begin
                if (pattern == 4'b0000) begin
                    next_state = COUNTDOWN;
                end
            end
            COUNTDOWN: begin
                if (count == 4'b0000 && counter == 10'd0) begin
                    next_state = WAIT_ACK;
                end
            end
            WAIT_ACK: begin
                if (ack) begin
                    next_state = IDLE;
                end
            end
        endcase
    end

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            count <= 4'b0000;
            counting <= 1'b0;
            done <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            case (state)

                        done <= 1'b1;
                    end
                end
                WAIT_ACK: begin

                        done <= 1'b0;
                    end
                end
            endcase
        end
    end

endmodule

module TopModule (
    input  wire clk,
    input  wire reset,
    input  wire data,
    output reg [3:0] count,
    output reg counting,
    output reg done,
    input  wire ack
);

    typedef enum reg [2:0] {
        IDLE,
        DETECT_PATTERN,
        SHIFT_DELAY,
        COUNTDOWN,
        WAIT_ACK
    } state_t;

    state_t state, next_state;
    reg [3:0] pattern;
    reg [3:0] delay;
    reg [9:0] counter;

    always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
        if (reset) begin
            state <= IDLE;
            pattern <= 4'b0000;
            delay <= 4'b0000;
            counter <= 10'd0;
            count <= 4'b0000;
            counting <= 1'b0;
            done <= 1'b0;
        end else begin
            state <= next_state;
            case (state)
                IDLE: begin
                    if (data) begin
                        pattern <= {pattern[2:0], data};
                    end else begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end
                end
                DETECT_PATTERN: begin
                    if (pattern == 4'b1101) begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end else begin
                        pattern <= {pattern[2:0], data};
                    end
                end
                SHIFT_DELAY: begin
                    delay <= {delay[2:0], data};
                end
                COUNTDOWN: begin
                    if (counter != 10'd0) begin
                        counter <= counter - 1'b1;
                    end
                    if (counter % 10'd1000 == 10'd0 && counter != 10'd0) begin
                        count <= count - 1'b1;
                    end
                end
                WAIT_ACK: begin
                    if (ack) begin
                        pattern <= 4'b0000;
                    end
                end
            endcase
        end
    end

    always @(*) begin
        next_state = state;
        case (state)
            IDLE: begin
                if (pattern == 4'b1101) begin
                    next_state = SHIFT_DELAY;
                end
            end
            SHIFT_DELAY: begin
                if (counter == 10'd0) begin
                    next_state = COUNTDOWN;
                    counter = (delay + 1'b1) * 10'd1000;
                    count = delay;
                    counting = 1'b1;
                end
            end
            COUNTDOWN: begin
                if (counter == 10'd0) begin
                    next_state = WAIT_ACK;
                    counting = 1'b0;
                    done = 1'b1;
                end
            end
            WAIT_ACK: begin
                if (ack) begin
                    next_state = IDLE;
                    done = 1'b0;
                end
            end
        endcase
    end

endmodule

module RefModule (
  input clk,
  input in,
  input reset,
  output out
);

  parameter A=0, B=1;
  reg state;
  reg next;

    always_comb begin
    case (state)
      A: next = in ? A : B;
      B: next = in ? B : A;
    endcase
    end

    always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (reset) state <= B;
        else state <= next;
  end

  assign out = (state==B);

endmodule
```

);

);

reg [15:0] q_reg;

always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (!resetn) begin
        q_reg <= 16'b0;
    end else begin
        if (byteena[0])
            q_reg[7:0] <= d[7:0];
        if (byteena[1])
            q_reg[15:8] <= d[15:8];
    end
end

assign q = q_reg;

endmodule
```

<details><summary>rtlforge/parsers.py — render_feedback</summary>

```python
 269  def render_feedback(diags: List[Diagnostic], limit: int = 12) -> str:
 270      """Compact, deduplicated feedback block for the repair prompt."""
 271      seen: set = set()
 272      lines: List[str] = []
 273      for d in diags:
 274          key = (d.severity, d.line, d.code, d.message)
 275          if key in seen:
 276              continue
 277          seen.add(key)
 278          lines.append("- " + d.render())
 279          if len(lines) >= limit:
 280              lines.append(f"- (further diagnostics omitted)")
 281              break
 282      return "\n".join(lines)
```

</details>

<details><summary>rtlforge/runners.py — lint</summary>

```python
 163  def lint(design: Path, workdir: Path, strict: bool = False) -> StageResult:
 164      """Verilator lint.
 165  
 166      strict=False treats warnings as advisory: style warnings like
 167      DECLFILENAME would otherwise send the model into pointless repair cycles
 168      on designs that are functionally fine. Set strict=True to study whether
 169      warning-level feedback helps or hurts -- that ablation is the interesting
 170      part.
 171      """
 172      if (miss := _missing("verilator", "lint")):
 173          return miss
 174  
 175      rc, out = _run(
 176          ["verilator", "--lint-only", "-Wall", design.name], workdir
 177      )
 178      if (why := infra_failure(rc, out)):
 179          return StageResult("lint", False, note=f"tool did not run: {why}",
 180                             raw=out)
 181      diags = parse_verilator(out)
 182      errors = [d for d in diags if d.severity == "error"]
 183      passed = not errors if not strict else not diags
 184      return StageResult("lint", passed, diags if not passed else [], out)
 185  
 186
```

 506      iterations = 1 if feedback_level == "none" else max_iterations
 507  
 508      for i in range(1, iterations + 1):
 509          result.iterations = i
 510          design.write_text(rtl)
 511          rtl_hash = hashlib.sha256(rtl.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
 512  
 513          # Keep every attempt, not just the one that ran last. The failed ones
 514          # are often the most informative: a timing run that plateaued showed
 515          # the model reaching its best structure immediately, then repeatedly
 516          # attempting something more ambitious and mis-writing it. That is only
 517          # visible if the broken attempts survive.
 518          (workdir / f"attempt_{i:02d}.v").write_text(rtl)
 519          attempt = Attempt(index=i, rtl=rtl, rtl_hash=rtl_hash,
 520                            from_timing_repair=last_was_timing_repair)
 521  
 522          # An identical regeneration means the model has stopped responding to
 523          # feedback. Further iterations cost tokens and change nothing.
 524          if rtl_hash in seen_hashes:
 525              attempt.failed_stage = "generate"
 526              attempt.stages.append({
 527                  "stage": "generate", "passed": False, "skipped": False,
 528                  "note": "identical to an earlier attempt; loop stalled",
 529                  "diagnostics": [{"severity": "error", "line": None,
 530                                   "code": "STALLED",
 531                                   "message": "regenerated identical RTL"}],
 532                  "metrics": None,
 533              })
 534              result.attempts.append(attempt)
 535              result.stop_reason = "stalled"

 537          seen_hashes.add(rtl_hash)

 539          # Cheap structural check before invoking any tool. Catches truncated
 540          # or empty generations with an unambiguous message.
 541          if f"module {problem.top}" not in rtl or "endmodule" not in rtl:
 542              note = ("response truncated at the token limit"
 543                      if client.last_finish_reason == "length"
 544                      else f"generated text does not define module "
 545                           f"`{problem.top}` with a matching endmodule")
 546              attempt.stages.append({
 547                  "stage": "generate", "passed": False, "skipped": False,
 548                  "note": note,
 549                  "diagnostics": [{"severity": "error", "line": None,
 550                                   "code": "TRUNCATED", "message": note}],
 551                  "metrics": None,
 552              })
 553              attempt.passed = False
 554              result.attempts.append(attempt)

<details><summary>rtlforge/loop.py — seen_behaviour[</summary>

````python
 609                  if sr.waveform:
 610                      feedback += (
 611                          "\n\nSignal trace around the first mismatch "
 612                          "(_ref is correct, _dut is yours):\n```\n"
 613                          + sr.waveform + "\n```"
 614                      )
 615                  break
 616              if stage == "synth" and sr.metrics:
 617                  result.final_cells = sr.metrics.cells
 618  
 619          attempt.passed = failed_stage is None
 620          attempt.failed_stage = failed_stage
 621          if failed_stage and attempt.stages:
 622              attempt.magnitude = _magnitude_from(attempt.stages[-1])
 623              attempt.behaviour = _behaviour_key(failed_stage, attempt.stages[-1])
 624          result.attempts.append(attempt)
 625  
 626          if attempt.passed:
 627              result.passed = True
 628              result.stop_reason = "passed"
 629              break
 630  
 631          # Three attempts with the same observable failure means the loop is
 632          # exploring rewordings, not fixes.
 633          if attempt.behaviour:
 634              seen_behaviour[attempt.behaviour] = \
 635                  seen_behaviour.get(attempt.behaviour, 0) + 1
 636              if seen_behaviour[attempt.behaviour] >= 3:
 637                  result.stop_reason = "behaviourally stalled"
 638                  break
 639          if feedback_level == "none":
 640              result.stop_reason = "single-shot"
 641              break
 642          if i == iterations:
 643              result.stop_reason = "iteration budget"
 644              break
 645  
 646          # A failure at a stage outside this level's repair set is terminal:
 647          # the configuration under test has no signal to act on. This is the
 648          # point of the ablation -- a lint-only agent genuinely cannot see a
 649          # functional bug, and must be scored as failing when it hits one.
 650          if failed_stage not in repair_stages:
 651              result.terminal_stage = failed_stage
 652              result.stop_reason = "no feedback signal for this stage"
 653              break
 654  
 655          # Roll back to the best attempt so far if this one regressed. Without
 656          # this the loop can repair a worse design into an even worse one --
 657          # observed as attempts that introduce new lint errors after previously
 658          # passing lint.
````

```python
  39  }
  40  
  41  
  42  @dataclass
  43  class Usage:
  44      prompt_tokens: int = 0
  45      completion_tokens: int = 0
  46  
  47      def __add__(self, other: "Usage") -> "Usage":
  48          return Usage(
  49              self.prompt_tokens + other.prompt_tokens,
  50              self.completion_tokens + other.completion_tokens,
  51          )
  52  
  53  
  54  class LLMError(RuntimeError):
  55      pass
  56  
  57  
  58  class LLMClient:
  59      def __init__(
  60          self,
  61          provider: str = "groq",
  62          model: Optional[str] = None,
  63          base_url: Optional[str] = None,
  64          temperature: float = 0.2,
  65          max_retries: int = 4,
  66          max_tokens: int = 8192,
  67          reasoning_effort: Optional[str] = None,
  68      ):
  69          if provider not in PROVIDERS:
  70              raise LLMError(
  71                  f"unknown provider {provider!r}; "
  72                  f"choose from {sorted(PROVIDERS)}"
  73              )
  74          default_url, default_model, key_env = PROVIDERS[provider]
  75  
  76          self.provider = provider
  77          self.base_url = (base_url or default_url).rstrip("/")
  78          self.model = model or default_model
  79          self.temperature = temperature
  80          self.max_retries = max_retries
  81          # Reasoning models spend budget before emitting code, so this must be
  82          # well above the size of the expected module. But it is also reserved
  83          # against tokens-per-minute at request time, so oversizing it can
  84          # cause 429s on an otherwise idle account. 8192 is the compromise.
  85          self.max_tokens = max_tokens
  86          # gpt-oss models spend most of their budget reasoning before emitting
  87          # any code -- 2500-4100 tokens for a ~600-token Verilog module. On a
  88          # capped free tier that is the difference between 50 generations a day
```

<details><summary>rtlforge/runners.py — simulate</summary>

```python
 213  def simulate(workdir: Path, timeout: int = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT,
 214               sim_format: str = "markers") -> StageResult:
 215      """Run the compiled simulation. Requires compile_rtl to have run with a tb.
 216  
 217      sim_format "markers"     -> our TB_PASS / MISMATCH protocol
 218      sim_format "verilogeval" -> "Mismatches: N in M samples"
 219      """
 220      if (miss := _missing("vvp", "simulate")):
 221          return miss
 222      if not (workdir / "sim.out").exists():
 223          return StageResult(
 224              "simulate", False, skipped=True,
 225              note="no sim.out; compile stage did not produce a binary",
 226          )
 227  
 228      rc, out = _run(["vvp", "sim.out"], workdir, timeout=timeout)
 229      if (why := infra_failure(rc, out)):
 230          return StageResult("simulate", False, note=f"tool did not run: {why}",
 231                             raw=out)
 232      parse = (parse_verilogeval_sim if sim_format == "verilogeval"
 233               else parse_simulation)
 234      passed, diags = parse(out)
 235  
 236      # On failure, pull the trace window around the divergence. "First mismatch
 237      # at time 130" tells the model when but not what; the window shows the
 238      # inputs that led there and how its output differed from the reference.
 239      wave = ""
 240      if not passed:
 241          vcd = workdir / "wave.vcd"
 242          if vcd.exists():
 243              wave = waveform.window_at(vcd, waveform.first_mismatch_time(out))
 244  
 245      return StageResult("simulate", passed, [] if passed else diags, out,
 246                         waveform=wave)
 247  
 248
```

</details>

 249  def synthesize(design: Path, workdir: Path, top: str) -> StageResult:
 250      """Yosys generic synthesis. Confirms synthesizability and yields area."""
 251      if (miss := _missing("yosys", "synth")):
 252          return miss
 253  
 254      script = f"read_verilog -sv {design.name}; synth -top {top}; stat"
 255      rc, out = _run(["yosys", "-p", script], workdir)
 256      if (why := infra_failure(rc, out)):
 257          return StageResult("synth", False, note=f"tool did not run: {why}",
 258                             raw=out)
 259      metrics, diags = parse_yosys(out)
 260      passed = rc == 0 and not diags
 261      return StageResult(
 262          "synth", passed, diags if not passed else [], out, metrics=metrics
 263      )
```
